tarumpeṟa lamiḻta maṉṉa
karumpivaṭ ṭantōṉ perumpiṟaṅ kaṭiyē	(puṟam. 392)
eṉṟu atiyamāṉ makaṉ pokuṭṭeḻiṉiyi auviyār pāṭi yiruppatāl, avaṉ muṉṉōruḷ oruvaṉ cīṉat tiliruntō cāvakattiliruntō, karumpit tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkuk koṇṭu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu.2 marutanilat teyvamākiya vēntaṉ eṉṉum intiraṉukkup pukārc cōḻar viḻāk koṇṭāṭiyatum, avaṉ uḻavut toḻiṟku vēṇṭum maḻivaḷan taruvaṉ eṉṉum kuṟikkōḷ paṟṟiyatē pōlum!
17
vaṇikamum pōkkuvarattum
uḻaviṉāl viḷikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷiyum kittoḻi lāṟ ceyyappaṭum poruḷkaḷiyum uḷnāṭṭiṟ pakiravum, uḷ nāṭṭup poruḷkaḷi veḷināṭṭilum veḷināṭṭup poruḷkaḷi uḷ nāṭṭilum parappavum, perum poruḷīṭṭi nāṭṭuc celvattip perukkavum, vaṇikam iṉṟiyamiyāta teṉṟu kaṇṭa paṇṭiyaracar, ataṉi ollum vāyellām ūkkivantaṉar.
nakarantoṟum vaṇikar kuḻumam (Merchant Guild) iruntatu. nāṭṭuppuṟa nakarāṭci perumpālum nakara vaṇikar kiyil iruntatu. nakaraṅkaḷiyellām aracaṉ nēraṭiyāka mēṟpārttu vantaṉaṉ. talimi vaṇikaṉukku eṭṭip paṭṭamum eṭṭippuravum aḷikkap paṭṭami muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. vaṇikar kuḻumaṅkaṭku valañci maṇikkirāmam añcuvaṇṇam mutaliya paṭṭaṅkaḷum aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.
varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉpē, nilavāṇikam nīrvāṇikam ākiya iruvaki vāṇikamum tamiḻakattil taḻittōṅki yiruntaṉa. nilaṅkaṭantu ceyyum nilavāṇikam kāliṟpirivu eṉṟum, nīrkaṭantu ceyyum nīr vāṇikam kalattiṟ pirivu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
munnīr vaḻakkam makaṭūuvō ṭilli	(980)
eṉṉun tolkāppiyac cūttirattāl, tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē nīrvāṇikam ciṟantiruntami aṟiyappaṭum.
nilappōkkuvarattu: taṭivaḻi yeṉṟum peruvaḻi yeṉṟum kūṟappaṭum cālikaḷ nāṭeṅkum iruntaṉa. avi 64 cāṇ akalamum irumaruṅkum maraṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉavāyiruntaṉa. kōṭ ṭāṟṟuc cāli, maturip peruvaḻi araṅkam nōkkip pōnta peruvaḻi koṅkuvaḻi taṭikivaḻi, vaṭukavaḻi mutaliya peyarkaḷāl, tamiḻnāṭṭup perunakarkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟit teṉṉāṭṭuc cīmikaḷum cālikaḷāl iṇikkappaṭṭiruntami pulaṉām. toṇṭināṭṭiṉ mēṟkelli oru peru vaḻiyāyirunta teṉpatu, mēṟkup peruvaḻiyām eṉṉum kampar kūṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum. āṅkāṅkuḷḷa ūraviyār avaravar ūrarukē yuḷḷa taṭivaḻip pakutikaḷik kavaṉittu vantaṉar.
vēṭṭikkucceṉṟaaracaṉneṭumāṉ ṟēroṭupākaṉinilavumaṇaṟkāṉyāṟṟuniṟkappaṇittu........ïU«bghÊšpukum”ednakkīra®kūṟiyiruppatāl,iyaṉwviṭamellā«caṅkakkāla¤taraca®tērēṟi¢ceṉṟaṉareṉṟum,ataṟkuriavaḻivacatikaŸamikka¥paṭṭiruttašvēṇṭumeṉṟu«ūkikkalām.
cālikaḷ piriyumiṭaṅkaḷil allatu cērumiṭaṅkaḷil, avvac cāli cellum ūrp peyar poṟitta vaḻikāṭṭi maraṅkaḷ nāṭṭap paṭṭiruntaṉa.
māṭu, kutiri, kōvēṟukaḻuti ākiya mūvakip poti mākkaḷiyuṭiya vaṇikaccāttukaḷ, aṭikkaṭi cālikaḷil iyaṅkikkoṇṭiruntaṉa.
kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum kuntiyaṭikaḷum aruvilic cilampuṭaṉ pukāriliruntu muppatu kātat toliviluḷḷa maturikkum, tēvantiyumkaṇṇakiyi‹ceviliyu«aṭittōḻiyu«pukārilirunJmatuuvaḻiyāŒvañcikkum,maṇimēkalapukāriliruªtuvañcikFmpiṉdrvañciyiliruªtukāñcikku«,māṭayṉpukāriṉi‹ṟukumarikFmpiṉṉa®kkaṅkikku«,parācuṉcōṇāṭṭiṉi‹ṟurranāṭṭiṟFmpi‹pupeUmporuḷulṉpāṇṭinā£ṭuvaḻiyāfttaṉṉūrukku«,yātēhriṭarppahṭumiṉ¿cceṉṟatāš,akkālatâlvaḻippōkkar¡kuvēṇLmvaḻivacatiímpātukāpòmnira«paviruntimayaṟiyappaṭu«.
tamiḻaracar aṭikkaṭi vaṭanāṭṭiṉmēṟ paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟatum; avaruḷ cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭanāṭṭuc celavil, 100 tērkaḷum 500 yāṉikaḷum 10,000 kutirikaḷum, 20,000 paṇṭa mēṟṟiya vaṇṭikaḷum, 1,000 caṭṭiyiṭṭa atikārikaḷum, māperun tokiyiṉarāṉa kālāṭ paṭiñarum, 102 nāṭaka makaḷirum, 208 kuyiluvarum, 100 naki vēḻamparum uṭaṉ ceṉṟa tum; akkālattirunta pōkkuvarattu vacatiyi oruvāṟuṇarttum.
aṉuppu eṉṟoru vari muṟkālattu vāṅkappaṭṭatiṉāl, orukāl añcal ēṟpāṭu akkālat tiruntirukkalām eṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu.
nīrp pōkkuvarattu: paḵṟuḷiyāṟu muḻuku muṉṉarē, kiḻakkilcāvakam maliyā kāḻakam (parmā) cīṉam mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷuṭaṉum, mēṟkil arapiyā pāpilōṉiyā kaltēyā mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷuṭaṉum, tamiḻakam vaṇikañ ceytu vantatu.
cīṉakkaṇṇāṭi cīṉakkāram cīṉakkiṇṇam cīṉaccūṭaṉ cīṉappaṭṭu mutaliyapaṇṭaṅkaḷcīṉattiṉiṉṟumilavaṅkappaṭṭi,kirāmpū,cātikkāy,cātippattirimutaliyacarakkukaḷnākanāṭukaḷeṉṉumkīḻintiyat ÔvukTṭṭattiṉiṉṟum,1jmiḻaracariṉelvakipgṭikaḷuḷxṉṟāṉaFtirippaṭikkunvaṇṭuṅFtirikaḷmrapiyāviṉiṉṟum,áttirappēḻigviviḷakkuktuKtaliyabpāruḷkaḷavaṉeṭukaḷiṉiṉṟumjmiḻakattiṟkukflaṅkaḷiltntiṟaṅkiṉa.nj¡F, tōki(mayil),arici,akil,cantaṉam,iñci,koṭṭi(pañcuccuruḷ),veṟṟili,aṭikkāy(pākku)mutaliyaglabpāruḷkaṭkutjmiḻpbpayarkaḷēnmalibmāḻifḷiltḻaṅkuvatu,gḻantamiḻeṭṭuVṟṟumaticáṟappikfṭṭum.fšnjah ehṭṭicrrntū®e‹ṉumilttilafḻnjṭukkappaṭṭoUtaÄḻnāṭṭutjkkavu¤taram,ki.K.3000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭatākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. ki.mu. 1000 āṇṭu kaṭku muṟpaṭṭa cālōmōṉ eṉṉum yūtavaracaṉ kālattil tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu yūtēyāviṟku ēṟṟumatiyāṉa tōkiyiṉ peyar yūta moḻiyāṉa epirēyattil tuki eṉa vaḻaṅki vantatu.
kaṭalvāṇikattip perukkutaṟporuṭṭu, ki.mu. 55-l pāṇṭiyaṉ  rōmavaracaṉukkum, ki.pi. 1015-l mutalām irācarācaṉum,   ki.pi. 1015-l irācēntiraṉum ki.pi. 1077- l mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉum cīṉavaracaṉukkum tūtu viṭuttaṉar.
muttamiḻ nāṭṭuk kaṭaṟkariyilum, oru kālattiṟku oṉṟum palavumāka, vevvēṟu kālattil vevvēṟu tuṟimukaṅkaḷ amintiruntaṉa. cōḻanāṭṭiṟku mayili, malli, pukār, kārikkāl, nāki, toṇṭi mutaliyaṉavum; pāṇṭināṭṭiṟkuk kavāṭam, koṟki, kāyal mutaliyaṉavum; cēranāṭṭiṟku vañci, muciṟi, toṇṭi, mānti, naṟavūr, koṭuṅkōḷūr, kāntaḷūr, viḻiñam, kōḻikkōṭu2 mutaliyaṉavum tuṟinakarkaḷāy iruntuvantaṉa.
tuṟimukantōṟum kalaṅkari viḷakkam (Light House) iruntatu. pukār kavāṭam vañci pōṉṟa talimi allatu kōnakart tuṟimukaṅkaḷil, palvēṟu nāṭṭuk kalaṅkaḷ palvakip paṇṭaṅkaḷi nāḷtōṟum ēṟṟuvatum, iṟakkuvatumā yiruntaṉa. ēṟṟumatiyum iṟakkumatiyumāṉa aḷaviṭappaṭāta paṇṭap potikaḷ. āyattiṟkāka niṟukkappaṭṭu avvat tamiḻnāṭṭaraca muttiri poṟikkappaṭṭapiṉ, tuṟimukattiviṭṭu nīṅkumvari ciṟanta kāvalceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. aṇiyavum cēyavumāṉa palvēṟu nāṭṭiliruntu vanta pulampeyar mākkaḷ taṅkuvataṟku, vacatiyāṉa cērikaḷum viṭutikaḷum iruntaṉa. tamiḻakak kīḻkaṭaṟ kariyil nākanāṭṭār kumari mutal vaṅkamvari pala nakaraṅkaḷil vantu taṅkiyiruntamiyāl, avi nākarkōyil, nākappaṭṭiṉam, nākūr, nākapuri eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. mīṉ piṭikkum kaṭṭumaram mutal kutirip paṭiyēṟṟat takka nāvāyvari palatarappaṭṭa marakkalaṅkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭu vaṇikarkkuc contamāyiruntaṉa. ēlēlaciṅkaṉ, kōvalaṉ muṉṉōr, paṭṭiṉattār eṉṉum tiruveṇkāṭar mutaliyōr peruṅkala vaṇikarāvar. ``ēlēla ciṅkaṉ kappal ēḻukaṭal ceṉṟālum mīḷum'' eṉpatu paḻamoḻi yākum.
cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ ``kaṭaṟkaṭam peṟinta kāvalaṉ'' eṉṟum, ``kaṭalpiṟakkōṭṭiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ'' eṉṟum pukaḻap peṟuvatāl akkālattaracar pakivarālum kaṭaṟkoḷḷik kārarālum viḷikkap paṭum tīṅkukaḷip pōkkik kaṭal vaṇikattik kāttami ūkikkappaṭum. pīlivaḷi nākanāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu pukār vantu mīḷavum, maṇimēkali īḻattiṟkum cāvakattiṟ kum ceṉṟu mīḷavum, vacatiyum pātukāvalumāṉa marakkalap pōkku varattu akkālattiruntatu.
nīriṉ vanta nimirparip puraviyuṅ
kāliṉ vanta karuṅkaṟi mūṭiyum
vaṭamalip piṟanta maṇiyum poṉṉuṅ
kuṭamalip piṟanta vāramu makilun
teṉkaṭal muttuṅ kuṇakaṭaṟ ṟukiruṅ
kaṅki vāriyuṅ kāvirip payaṉu
mīḻat tuṇavuṅ kāḻakat tākkamu
mariyavum periyavu neriya vīṇṭi	(185-192)
eṉṉum paṭṭiṉappālip pakutiyāl, pukārukku vantu cērnta iruvaki vaṇikap poruṭperukki oruvāṟuṇaralām.
18
kaliyuṅ kalviyum
paṇṭit tamiḻakattil kalvi ikkālattiṟpōl araciyal tiṇikkaḷaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāka iruntatilli. āciriyariṉ muyaṟci yiṉālum potumakḷiṉ pōṟṟaraviṉālumē, potukkalvi nāṭṭiṟ parappappeṟṟu vantatu. kaṟṟuvalla pulavariyē aracar ōraḷavu pōṟṟi vantaṉar.
potukkalvi: eḻuttum eṇṇum kaṟpitta tuvakkak kalviyāciriyarkku iḷampālāciriyar eṉṟum, ciṟṟilakkaṇamum nikaṇṭum kaṇakkuṅ kaṟpitta naṭuttarak kalviyāciriyarkkuk kaṇakkāyar eṉṟum, aintilakkaṇamum avaṟṟiṟkuriya ilakkiya muṅ kaṟpitta mēṟṟarak kalviyāciriyarkku āciriyar eṉṟum peyar. āciriyar, nūlāciriyar nuvalāciriyar (pōtakāciriyar) uriyāciriyar eṉa mūvakiyar.
tuvakkakkalvi māṇavarkkum naṭuttarak kalvi māṇavarkkum, paḷḷippiḷḷikaḷ eṉṟum, māṇiyar eṉṟum māṇavar (māṇavakar, māṇākkar) eṉṟum, caṭṭar eṉṟum peyar. avaruḷ talimi yāṉavaṉ caṭṭanampi allatu caṭṭanampip piḷḷi (caṭṭāmpiḷḷi) eṉappaṭṭāṉ. mēṟṟarak kalvi māṇavarkku māṇavar eṉṟum maḻapulavar eṉṟum, kaṟṟuccolliyar eṉṟum peyar.
tuvakkakkalvi niliyilum naṭuttarakkalvi niliyilum māṇavariṉ peṟṟōrum, mēṟṟarak kalvi niliyil māṇavarum, āciriyarkkuc campaḷam iṟuttu avarip pōṟṟi vantaṉar. ēḻiyāyiruntu āciriyaṉukkut toṇṭu ceyyum māṇavaṉukku muttarak kalviyum ilavacamāyk kaṟpikkappaṭṭatu.
ciṟappuk kalvi: māṇavar potukkalvi kaṟṟapiṉ, ici nāṭakam kaṇiyam maruttuvam mutaliya ciṟappuk kalikaḷi, avvak kali yāciriyar vāyilāyk kaṟṟaṉar. akkālattu nūlkaḷellām ceyyuḷ vaṭiviliruntamiyiṉ evvaki nūṟkalvikkum ciṟanta ilakkaṇak kalvi iṉṟiyamiyātatāyiruntatu.
poruḷ koṭuppōrkkum paṇiviṭi ceyvōrkkum kalip payiṟci yaḷikkappaṭṭatu.
meynnūṟ kalvi: potukkalvi kaṟṟapiṉ, allatu potukkalviyuñ ciṟappukkalviyuṅ kaṟṟapiṉ, meypporuḷaṟivu peṟa virumpiṉavar, kāṭṭakattuḷḷa muṉivariṭam allatu yōkiyariṭam ceṉṟu mūvāṇṭu meynnūṟ kalvi kaṟṟatākat terikiṉṟatu.
vēṇṭiya kalvi yāṇṭumūṉ ṟiṟavātu	(1134)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
ceyyuḷiyaṟṟum tiṟam: eṇṇum eḻuttum tavira evvakik kalviyum ceyyuḷ vāyilākavē kaṟṟamiyālum, iḷamiyi liruntu ceyyuḷ ceytum ceyyuḷiṟ pēciyum payiṉṟamiyālum, paṇṭip pulavarellām ceyyuḷiyaṟṟun tiṟam ciṟakkap peṟṟiruntaṉar.
aracar tamiḻi vaḷarttalum pulavarip pōṟṟalum: pāṇṭiyar toṉṟutoṭṭuk ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari kaḻakam (caṅkam) irutti, nūlniliyam pōṟṟi, 1 mutunūlārāycciyum putunūlākkamum nikaḻvittu, muttamiḻi vaḷarttu vantaṉar. kaḻakaṅ kulintapiṉ, muttamiḻaracarum tattam aviyil ōriru ciṟanta pulavari yamarttip pōṟṟi vantaṉar. avikkaḷap pulavarkkum, nūlaraṅkēṟiyavarkkum, aracarip pukaḻntu pāṭiyavarkkum muṟṟūṭṭum ciṟanta paricum aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. āṭal pāṭal vallārum araṅkēṟi āyiratteṇ kaḻañcupoṉ peṟṟami muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. araṅkēṟṟam aracaṉ muṉṉiliyilum avvat tamiḻiṟ ciṟanta atikāriyiṉ talimiyilum naṭipeṟ ṟatu. araṅkēṟiya nūlāciriyari veṇpaṭṭaṇivittu yāṉimē lēṟṟi nakarvalam varuvippatu vaḻakkam.
ilakkiyap pulavarum camayanūṟ pulavarum coṟpōr nikaḻtti uṇmi nāṭṭaṟkut, talinakartoṟum paṭṭimaṇṭapam eṉṉum tarukkamaṇṭapam iruntatu. pulavariṭit teyvappulamiyiyum piṟaruḷḷak karuttaṟiyum āṟṟaliyum vaḷarttaṟporuṭṭu, aracar avvappōtu tam uḷḷattiluḷḷa kiḷarccimikka karuttippaṟṟi avarip pāṭaccolvatuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉam pāṭappaṭuvatu kaṇṭa citti eṉappaṭṭatu. aracaruṭiya uḷḷakkarutti aṟivippatum aiyuṟavit teḷivippatumāṉa pāṭaṟkup poṟkiḻiyaḷikkappaṭṭatu. 
ittakiya camiyaṅkaḷil, iṅṅaṉam pāṭiṉārkkitu veṉṟu viḷampi muṉṉarē poṟkiḻiyic caṅkamaṇṭapattil tūkki viṭuvatu pāṇṭiyar vaḻakkam.
kuṟikki (camaciyi) pāṭalum, arikaṇṭam emakaṇṭam pōṉṟa kaṭumpā (ācukavi) pāṭalum, aracar avvappōtu nallicip pulavarkkum kaṭum pāvalarkkum naṭatti vanta ceyyuṭ pōṭṭi vakikaḷākum.
takka pulavar talimiyamiccarākavum tūtarākavum amarttap peṟṟaṉar. avikkaḷat talimip pulavarkkum, araṅkēṟiya kuṭikoṭi mutaliya koṟṟac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. pulavariṉ parinturikkum vēṇṭukōṭkum iṇaṅkuvatum, avaratu aṟivuriyiyum aṟavuriyiyum iṭitturiyiyum ēṟṟuk koḷvatum, avaratu pulanturiyiyum paḻippuriyiyum poṟuttuk koḷvatum, cevviyaṟiyātum viṭipeṟātum eccamayattilum ōlakka maṇṭapattiṟkuḷ avarip pukaviṭuvatum, avarukkup paṇiviṭi ceyvatum, avari vaḻiviṭukkumpōtu ēḻaṭi piṉpōy mīḷvatum, akkālat taracar palar ceyalkaḷākum.
paricaḷiyāviṭattum tammi avamatittapōtum pulavar aracaric cāvippatumuṇṭu.
kalvittara vuyarvu: akkālap pulavar kaṭuttup pāṭum āṟṟaluṭiyarāyiruntu ceyyuḷilēyē tammuḷ uriyāṭi vantaṉar. nikaṇṭu eṉṉum akarātiyuṭpaṭa aṉittilakkiyamum ceyyuḷ vaṭivilēyē iruntaṉa. maturic caṅkattilum piṟa pulavaraviyilum araṅkēṟiya piṉṉarē nūlkaḷ nāṭṭil ulavalāyiṉa.
kalvi vaḷarcci: taliccaṅkattil irunta pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭup pāṭiyavar nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avarip pōṟṟiya pāṇṭiyaruḷ kaviyaraṅkēṟiyavar eḻuvareṉṟum; iṭiccaṅkattil irunta pulavar aimpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭup pāṭiyavar mūvāyiratteḻunūṟṟuvar eṉṟum, avarip pōṟṟiya pāṇṭiyaruṭ kaviyaraṅkēṟiyavar aivar eṉṟum; kaṭic caṅkattil irunta pulavar  nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭup pāṭiyavar nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉ mar eṉṟum, avarip pōṟṟiya pāṇṭiyaruḷ kaviyaraṅkēṟiyavar mūvar eṉṟum muccaṅka varalāṟu kūṟum.
itupōtuḷḷa kaṭiccaṅka ilakkiyattiliruntu aṟiyap paṭum pulavar ēṟattāḻa ēḻunūṟṟuvar āvar. avaruḷ makaḷir irupat tiruvarum aracar paṉṉiruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭavarum āvar.
aṟuvivāṇikaṉ, ōlikkaṭikāraṉ, kaṇiyaṉ, kūttaṉ, kūlavāṇikaṉ, pāṇaṉ, porunaṉ, maruttuvaṉ, vaṇṇakkaṉ mutaliya palvakit toḻilāḷarum; kāvaṟ peṇṭu, araci, kuṟamakaḷ, pēymakaḷ mutaliya palatara makaḷirum pulavarāyiruntaṉar.
kaṭic caṅkattiṟkup piṟkālattilum, cēramāṉ perumāḷ nāyaṉārum kulacēkarāḻvārum kaṇṭarātittarum ativīrarāma pāṇṭiyaṉum pōlum aracarum, tiruveṇkāṭarpōlum vaṇikarum, 2ām auviyārum kārikkālammiyārum pōlum makaḷirum palar pulavarāyiruntaṉar.
varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta pulavar, caṅkakālattilum ataṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum ettuṇiyarō aṟiyōm.
ācukavikaḷ kācu eṉṟu piṟkālattil vāṅkappaṭṭa vari, aracavoppam peṟṟu avar vāḻkkic celaviṟku vāṅkap paṭṭatu pōlum!
aracar ilakkiyat toṇṭu: aracar pulavari nūliyaṟṟu māṟu ūkkiyum tāmum nūliyaṟṟiyum vantatuṭaṉ, pala taṉip pāṭalkaḷiyum pāvum poruḷum aḷavum paṟṟit tokuttum tokuppittum uḷḷaṉar, naṟṟiṇi tokuppittōṉ pāṇ ṭiyaṉ paṉṉāṭu tanta māṟaṉvaḻuti; kuṟuntoki tokuttōṉ pūrikkō; aiṅkuṟunūṟu tokuppittōṉ yāṉikkaṭcēy māntarañ cēralirumpoṟi; akanānūṟu tokuppittōṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ ukkirapperuvaḻuti.
19
camayamum koḷkiyum
immi maṟumi nalaṅkaṭkum vīṭṭiṉpattiṟkum iṟi vaṉaruḷ iṉṟiyamiyātateṉṉuṅ koḷkipaṟṟi paṇṭit tamiḻaracar tamakkum tam kuṭikaṭkum nalam varuvittaṟporuṭṭut tamakkicintatoru camayattik kaṭippiṭittu iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar.
tamiḻaracaruṅ kuṭikaḷum mutalāvatu aintiṇit teyvaṅ kaḷi vaṇaṅki vantaṉar. kuṟiñcikkuc cēyōṉ eṉṉum murukaṉum, mullikku māyōṉ eṉṉun tirumālum, marutattiṟku vēntaṉ eṉṉum intiraṉum, neytaṟku varuṇaṉ (vāraṇaṉ) eṉṉum kaṭalōṉum, pālikku māyōḷ eṉṉuṅ kāḷi (koṟṟavi)yum, teyvamāyiruntaṉar.
varuṇa vaṇakkam nāḷaṭivil niṉṟuviṭṭatu. intira vaṇakkam kaṭiccaṅkat tiṟutivariyiruntatu. maṟṟa mutteyva vaṇakkamum neṭukalun toṭarntatu.
kaṭiccaṅka kālattil, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattilum maturi yilum vañciyilum paṟpala teyvaṅkaṭkuk kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉa. pukārilirunta kōyilkaḷ kaṟpakak kōyil, veḷyāṉik kōyil, palatēvar kōyil, katiravaṉ kōyil, kayilikkōyil, murukaṉ kōyil, vaccirappaṭikkōyil, cāttaṉkōyil, arukaṉ kōyil, nilāk kōyil, civaṉkōyil, tirumāl kōyil mutayaṉa. murukaṉ, civaṉ, tirumāl, palatēvaṉ ākiya nālvarkkum ellāt talinakarkaḷilum kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉa.
teyvaṅkaḷ perunteyvam eṉṟum ciṟuteyvam eṉṟum iruvakippaṭum. camayat teyvaṅkaḷ perunteyvamum maṟṟavi ciṟuteyvamumākum. civam viṇavam ārukatam puttam piramam ulakāyatam eṉṉum āṟum kaṭiccaṅka kālattu aṟu camayam eṉṉalām. akam akappuṟam puṟam puṟappuṟam eṉa nālāṟu camayamākap pakuttuk kūṟiyatu piṟkālattatākum, civavaḻipāṭum mālvaḻipāṭum toṉṟutoṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭiṟkuriyavātaliṉ av viraṇṭum paṟṟiya civamum viṇavamum tamiḻaracarāl perumpālum kaṭip piṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.   av viraṇṭaṉuḷḷum civaneṟiyē aracariṭic ciṟantiruntatu. iṟivaṉ eṉṟum piṟavā yākkip periyōṉ eṉṟum civaṉukku vaḻaṅkiya peyarkaḷē, civaneṟit talimiyi uṇartti viṭum.
ciṟuteyvaṅkaḷ, kāṟṟum neruppum mutaliya iyaṟkit teyvamum cūraramakaḷum pūtamum mutaliya pēytteyvamum, katiravaṉun tiṅkaḷum mutaliya kōḷteyvamum, paṭṭa maṟavaṉākiya naṭukalteyvamum, iṟanta kaṟpiyākiya pattiṉitteyvamum, nālvaki varuṇattiṟkuriya varuṇatteyvamum, āṟum, maliyum nakarum mutaliya iṭattiṟkuriya iṭatteyvamum, kalviyum kātalum pōrum mutaliya toḻiṟkuriya toḻiṟṟeyvamum, kāppumaṭṭum aḷippatākak karutappaṭum kāpput teyvamum eṉap palavakiyām.
kaṭiccaṅka kālamvari, aracarum pulavarum potumakkaḷu mākiya muttiṟattāriṭiyum, camaviyaṟ koḷkiyum piṟar matap poṟiyum iruntuvantaṉa. ataṉpiṉ camayap pōr tamiḻnāṭṭil tali tūkkiṟṟu. mutalāvatu, vitika camayam eṉappaṭum civa viṇavaṅ kaṭkum avitika camayam eṉappaṭum camaṇa puttaṅ kaṭkum iṭiyē pōr eḻuntatu. piṉṉarc camaṇaputtaṅkaḷ oṭukkappaṭṭapiṉ, civa viṇavaṅkaṭkiṭiyē pōr mūṇṭatu.
caṅkakkālat taracarum mutalām irācarācaṉ irācēntiraṉ 
mutaliya piṟkālat taracarum, tām kaṭippiṭitta camayak kōyil kaṭku maṭṭumaṉṟip piṟa matakkōyilkaṭkum māṉiyam viṭṭaṉar. āyiṉ 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉaṉum camaṇaṉumāṉa makēntiravarmap pallavaṉ civa camaya kuravaruḷ oruvarāṉa tirunāvukkaracarip palavakiyil vatittuk kolla muyaṉṟāṉ. atē nūṟṟāṇṭiṉa ṉum civaṉaṭiyāruḷ oruvaṉumāṉa neṭumāṟaṉ eṇṇāyiram camaṇarik kaḻuvēṟṟiṉāṉ. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉaṉum civaṉu māṉa 2ām kulōttuṅkaṉ, kūrat tāḻvāṉ periyanampi eṉṉum iru viṇava kuravari muṟiyē kaṇ paṟittuc cākaṭittāṉ. atōṭu tilli maṉṟilirunta tirumāluru vattiyum peyarttuk kaṭalil eṟintuviṭṭāṉ. aṅṅaṉam orucila aracar neṟi tiṟampi naṭantaṉar. tirumaṅkiyāḻvār nākiyilirunta poṟ putta vuruvik koḷḷiyaṭittut tiruvaraṅkak kōyilip putuppit tāreṉṟu, oru paravi vaḻakkuc ceyti vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
20
tiruppaṇikaḷ
immi maṟumi vīṭeṉṉum mummi yiṉpattiṟkum iṟivaḻi pāṭum iṉṟiyamiyātatākak karutappaṭṭamiyiṉ paṇṭit tamiḻakattil iṅkilāntiṟpōl matattuṟiyum ōr araciyal tiṇik kaḷamāka iruntuvantatu. piṟa matak kōyilkaṭkum iṭiyiṭi māṉiyam viṭappaṭṭatēṉum, civa viṇavak kōyilkaḷē perumpālum aracariṉ āṭcikkuṭ paṭṭiruntaṉa.
pēraracarellārum oṉṟum palavumākap peruṅkōyilkaḷ kaṭṭi vittaṉar. ki.pi. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta kōcceṅkaṭ cōḻaṉ eḻupatu civaṉ kōyilkaḷik kaṭṭiṉāṉ. ki. pi. 10ām 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta mutalām irācarācaṉ tañcip peruvuṭiyār kōyilik kaṭṭi viliyēṟappeṟṟa aṇikalaṅ kaḷ ūrtikaḷ taṭṭu muṭṭukaḷuṭaṉ 35 ūrkaḷit tēvatāṉamāka viṭṭāṉ. kōyil kaṭṭāta aracar iṟiyiliyum aṇikalaṅkaḷum piṟavum aḷittaṉar.
kōyiṟku kōṭṭam, kuṭici, tēvakulam, taḷi, nakaram, paḷḷi, mantiram, māḷiki, ampalam mutaliya pala peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. kōyilkaḷ ciṟukōyil peruṅkōyil eṉa irutiṟattaṉa. ciṟu kōyilkaḷi ūraviyārum peruṅkōyilkaḷit taṉik kuḻuvārum kavaṉittu vantaṉar. peruṅkōyili mēṟpārp pavarkkuk kōyil vāriyattār eṉṟum srīkāriyam pārppār eṉṟum, avaruḷ talivaṉukku srīkāriyak kaṇkāṇi nāyakam eṉṟum peyar. civaṉ kōyili mēṟpārppavar makēcuvarar eṉṟum, tirumāl kōyili mēṟpārppavar mākaviṇavar allatu tāṉattār eṉṟum potuvākak kūṟap peṟuvar. kōyiṟ kāriyaṅkaḷi naṭattivippataṟku āṉavāḷ eṉṟōr araciyal viṉiṉum iruntāṉ.
kōyiṟ pūcārikkut tiruvaṭi piṭippāṉ eṉṟum, pōṟṟi eṉṟum, nampiṉāṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ tuṇivaṉukku eṭuttuk kinīṭṭi eṉṟum, peyaruṇṭu.1 ivar aṇukkat toṇṭarāvar. ivarallātu tirukkik kōṭṭi yōtuvār, tēvaraṭiyār (patiyilār), cākkiyaṉ, paṇṭāri, aṟikāraṉ, tēvakaṉmikaḷ, meḻukkaṭikaḷ, tiruviḷakkiṭuvār, tirumeykkāppu (tirumeykāp pōṉ, tirumēṉi kāval) mutaliya ettuṇiyō viṉiñar kōyilic cērntiruntaṉar. perunāṭu muḻutumirunta tirukkik kōṭṭi yōtuvārkkut tēvāra nāyakam eṉṟoru taliva ṉiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
iṟiyili (tēvatāṉam) , naṉkoṭi, nērttikkaṭaṉ, kāṇikki, vari, taṇṭam, paṟimutal ākiya eḻuvakiyil kōyiṟku varumāṉam vantukoṇṭiruntatu.
kōyiṟ celaviṟkellām potuppaṭa viṭappaṭṭirunta iṟiyiliyuṭaṉ, vevvēṟu celaviṟkut taṉittaṉi avvappōtu aracarālum perumakkaḷālum viṭappaṭṭu vanta iṟiyilikaḷum uḷa. avi tiruviḻāppuṟam, kaṟṟaḷippuṟam, nantavaṉappuṟam, aṭukkaḷippuṟam, avalamutuppuṟam, akkāravaṭici lamutuppuṟam, meḻukkaṭippuṟam, tiruvaṉantaṟ kaṭṭaḷi mutaliyaṉa. kōyiṟ paṇiyāḷarkkup pakirappaṭum tiruccōṟṟiṟkāka viṭappaṭum kaṭṭaḷi aṭṭiṟpēṟu eṉappaṭṭatu. oruvar ciṟitukālam nukarntapiṉ kōyiṟku viṭumāṟu aḷikkappaṭum nilattiṟku iṟik kaṭṭaḷi eṉṟu peyar.
kōyilil viḷakkēṟṟuvataṟkum piṟa tiruppaṇikkaṭkum pārppaṉari uṇpippataṟkum, vēṟu vēṟāka, aracarālum peru makkaḷālum, potumakkaḷālum kōyilil nitikaḷum toki kaḷum oppaṭikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ vaṭṭiyik koṇṭē ap paṇikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭu vantamiyiṉ, am mutalkaḷ aḻiyā tiruntaṉa. vaṭṭi polici eṉappaṭṭatu. tām kuṟitta tiruppaṇi kaḷum aṟaṅkaḷum nāḷtōṟum allatu niliyāka naṭipeṟaṟ poruṭṭu, avaṟṟip policiyik koṇṭē naṭattavēṇṭu meṉṟu, koṭiyāḷarē kuṟittuviṭuvatu marapu.
paṟimutalāṉa kuṟṟavāḷikaḷiṉ cottukaḷ kōyiṟku viṟkappaṭṭu, araciṟi niluvipōka eñciyatellām, kōyiloṭu cē rkkappaṭṭatu.
kōyil varumāṉattil celavupōka eccamellām aṟamuṅ
kalviyumākiya potunalap paṇikkuc celaviṭappaṭṭatu. 
akkālattuk kōyilkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu pala tuṟiyil potunalat toṇṭāṟṟi vantaṉa.
(1) toḻiṟ ṟuṟi: kōyil nilaṅkaḷ ēḻi yuḻavariṭattum, kōyiṟ kaṉṟukālikaḷ ēḻi yiṭiyariṭattum, vārac cākupaṭik kum vaḷarppiṟkum viṭappaṭṭaṉa.
cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ kālattil, cūralūri yaṭuttirunta kōyiṟ kuḷamum vāykkālum aṇiyum, piḷḷiyāṉ eṉṉum cempaṭavaṉ pārviyilum, avaṟṟiṉ paḻutupārppu cūralūr veṭṭiyāṉiṭattum, viṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. piḷḷiyāṉukkuk kōyiṟkuṭikaḷ celuttum vāykkāl pāṭṭamum pācip
pāṭṭamum, veṭṭiyāṉukkuc campaḷamum umpaḷamum, kim
māṟāka aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.2 kaṇkāṇippu, vaḻipāṭu, maṭit
toḻil, tuppuravākkam, alaṅkarippu, kaṇakku, kāval mutaliya paltuṟipaṟṟiya aluvalāḷa rōṭu, icivāṇarum kūttarum kammiyarum paṇimakkaḷum kuṭimakkaḷu māka ettuṇiyō pēr kōyilil niliyāka amarttappeṟṟut takka campaḷam peṟṟu vantaṉar.
tañcip peruvuṭiyār kōyilil, tēvāra ōtuvār nāṟpatteṇmarum patiyilār nāṉūṟṟuvarum, icikkaruvi
yiyakkuvār paṟpalarum, cākkiyarum, kaṇiyarum, aivakik kollarum, kuyavarum, tiyaṟkārarum, vaṇṇārum, muṭiyalaṅkāra viṉiñarum uṭpaṭa ēṟattāḻa āyiravar niliyāṉa viṉiñarāka amarttap peṟṟiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. pañcakālattum piṟa kālattum, uṇaviṟku vaḻiyaṟṟa āṭavarum peṇṭirum kōyiṟ kaṭimi pukkuk kōyiṟpaṇiyāṟ piḻittuvantaṉar. avarukku maṇakkavum kuṭumpa vāḻkki naṭattavum urimiyiruntatu.
(2) paṇattuṟi: paṇam vēṇṭiyavarkku, vaṭṭik kīṭākak kōyilil kuṟippiṭṭa viḷakkēṟṟumāṟu, kōyiṟ paṇṭārat tiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṭukkappaṭṭatu.
varakuṇapāṇṭiyaṉāl, tiruccentiṟ kōyil nitta vaḻipāṭṭiṟkāka 1400  poṟkācu paṉṉīrūrāriṭip pakirntu   koṭukkappaṭṭatu. vaṭṭi āṇṭiṟku oru kāciṟku irukalam nel eṉṟum, vaṭṭiyik koṇṭu vaḻipāṭṭi naṭappikkavēṇṭu meṉṟum, vaṭṭi niluviyāyiṉ iraṭṭiyum 25 kācu taṇṭamum iṟukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum vitikkappaṭṭaṉa. 3
aṟattiṟkum kaṭṭaḷikkum kōyiṟkuk koṭukkappaṭum paṇat tokikaḷ, kōyilatikārikaḷiṭamāvatu, ūraviyāriṭa māvatu, nakarattāriṭamāvatu, taṉippaṭṭavariṭamāvatu oppaṭik kappaṭuvatuṇṭu.
(3) kalvittuṟi: eṇṇāyiram eṉṟa iṭattil oru maṟi nūl ( vēta) viṭutik kallūriyum, tirumukkūṭal eṉṉum iṭattil oru vaṭamoḻi viṭutik kallūriyum tiruvoṟṟiyūril viyākaraṇa tāṉa maṇṭapam eṉṉum vaṭamoḻip pāṇiṉiyilakkaṇak kallūriyum, kōyilōṭu cērkkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. eṇṇāyirattil 500 māṇavarum 10 āciriyarum iruntaṉar. cila iṭaṅkaḷil vāṉanūlum tarukkamum pirapākaram eṉṉum mīmāmci nūlum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kaṟpikkappaṭṭaṉa. māṇavarkkuk kalviyum ūṇuṭiyuṟiyuḷum ilavacamāy aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. ataṟkup pōtiya iṟiyilikaḷ viṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
pala iṭaṅkaḷil itikāca purāṇaṅkaḷ potumakkaṭkut tamiḻil viritturikkappaṭṭaṉa. ataṟkum iṟiyili viṭappaṭṭi runtatu.
(4) kalittuṟi: ici kūttu ōviyam ciṟpam vaṇṇam mutaliya pala kalikaḷ, kōyil vāyilāy vaḷarkkappeṟṟaṉa.
(5) varalāṟṟut tuṟi: kalveṭṭukaḷ, kōyil,maṇṭapam, pāṟi, veṟṟittūṇ, malikkuki, paṭimi, naṭukal mutaliya palaviṭaṅkaḷil veṭṭappaṭṭaṉavēṉum, perumpāṉmiyākak kōyilkaḷilēyē avi iṭampeṟṟaṉa.
ki.pi. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa tamiḻaracar varalāṟṟi 
aṟitaṟku uṟutuṇiyāṉavi kalveṭṭukaḷē. aracaruṭiya pōrc  ceyalkaḷiyum veṟṟiyiyuṅ kūṟum. meykkīrttikaḷum, ūravi yārukkum nāṭṭatikārikaṭkum piṟarkkum viṭukkappaṭṭa aracāṇi kaḷum, ūraviyārāl aṉṟaṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭa putut tīrmāṉaṅkaḷum, aracarālum atikārikaḷālum perumakkaḷālum koṭukkappaṭṭa tāṉap paṭṭayaṅkaḷum kallil veṭṭappaṭṭuk kōyilil ellāruṅ kāṇu miṭattiṟ patikkappeṟuvatu, tavirā vaḻakkamāyiruntatu.
oru citinta kōyilip putuppikkumpōtu, ūraviyār nāṭṭatikāri vāyilāy aracaṉukkaṟivittu avaṉatu icivu peṟṟa piṉ, kōyililuḷḷa kalveṭṭukaḷi ``icciri vimāṉamiṭittuk kaṭṭuṅkāl kaṇṭa cilālēkippaṭi'' eṉṟu toṭaṅki ōlip paṭi yeṭuttu, putuk kalveṭṭup poṟittu, kōyil kaṭṭi muṭintapiṉ, ap putuk kalveṭṭukaḷi atikārikaḷ kāṭṭu miṭattil patippatu vaḻakkam. kalveṭṭup paṭiyeṭuppu kaṟpaṭi māṟṟu eṉappaṭṭatu. citintupōṉa kalveṭṭukaḷip putup pikkumpōtum immuṟiyē kiyāḷap peṟum.
(6) aṟattuṟi: pārppaṉari yuṇpikkum akkira
cāliyum, aṭiyāri yuṇpikkum maṭamum, irappōri yuṇpikkum aṭiciṟ cāliyum ākiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟō palavō, pala vūrkaḷiṟ kōyilō ṭiṇikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. perum
paṟṟappuliyūril irunta aṟaccāli aṟapperuñ celvi eṉṟum, tirumuk kūṭalil cāttirar eṉṉum pārppaṉa vaṭamoḻi māṇavarkku maruttakamākavum maruttuvak kallūriyākavu mirunta āyurvēta maruttuvacāli (ātulacāli) `vīracōḻaṉ' eṉṟum peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa.
(7) araciyaṟ ṟuṟi: araciṟi yiṟukkāta nilaṅkaḷum ūrkaḷum kōyiṟku viṟkappaṭṭu, av vilit tokiyiṉiṉṟu avaṟṟiṟkuriya araciṟi yiṟukkappaṭṭatu,
ūr maṇṭapamillāta vūrkaḷilellām, araciyaṟ kūṭṭaṅkaḷ kōyil maṇṭapattilēyē kūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. 
cila aracar kōyiṟpaṇiyoṭu tirunūṟpaṇiyum ceytu vantaṉar. mutalām irācarācaṉ tirumuṟi kaṇṭatum, mūṉṟām kulōttuṅkaṉ periya purāṇam iyaṟṟuvittatum nūṟpaṇiyām.
kōyiṟ kaṇakkukaḷ āṇṭutōṟum nāṭṭatikārikaḷāl taṇikki ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ taṇṭikkappaṭṭaṉar.
21
aracar viḻākkaḷ
aracar viḻākkaḷ potuvum ciṟappum eṉa irupāṟpaṭum. avaṟ ṟuṭ potuvāvaṉa :
(1) maṇṇumaṅkalam: aracaṉ mutaṉ muṟiyāka muṭicūṭum pōtum, āṇṭutoṟum varum muṭicūṭiya nāḷilum, koṇṭāṭap peṟum viḻā maṇṇumaṅkalam ākum. aḵtu nīrāṭi muṭicūṭum maṅkala viṉiyātalāl maṇṇumaṅkalam eṉappaṭṭatu. maṇṇutal - nīrāṭutal. tolkāppiyar itaṉic
ciṟanta cīrtti maṇṇumaṅkalam eṉpar 	(1037)
uḻiñip pōril, matiṟkaṇ ōraracaṉ maṟṟō raracaṉik koṉṟu avaṉ kuṭikkalam mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu, paṭṭa vēntaṉ peyarāl muṭipuṉintu nīrāṭum maṅkalam,
`kuṭumi koṇṭa maṇṇumaṅkalam' 	(tol.1014)
eṉappaṭum. atu akattōṉ maṇṇumaṅkalamum puṟattōṉ maṇṇumaṅkalamum eṉa iruvaki.
māṟṟaracaṉ matiliyaḻittuk kaḻutiyērāl uḻutu veḷḷi varakuṅ koḷḷuṅ kavaṭiyum vitti, amaṅkalamāyiṉa ceyta veṟṟivēntaṉ avaṟṟiṟkuk kaḻuvāyāka nīrāṭum maṅkalam, 
`maṉṉeyil aḻitta maṇṇu maṅkalam'		(tol. 1037) 
eṉappaṭum.
(2) perumaṅkalam: āṇṭutoṟuṅ koṇṭāṭap peṟum aracaṉ piṟantanāṭ koṇṭāṭṭam perumaṅkalam ākum. atu perunāḷ eṉavum paṭum. atil aracaṉ uyirkaḷiṭattuk kāṭṭum aruṭkaṟikuṟiyākat tūya veḷḷaṇi yaṇintu, ciṟippaṭṭavari viṭutali ceytu, koliyuñ ceruvum oḻintu, iṟitavirtalum tāṉañceytalum piṟavum mēṟkoḷvatu vaḻakkam.
(3) veṟṟi viḻā: aracaṉ pōril peṟṟa veṟṟiyit taṉṉakarilāyiṉum, māṟṟāṉ nakarilāyiṉum, īriṭattumāyiṉum, koṇṭāṭuvatu veṟṟiviḻāvākum. itiyoṭṭi, ampalam poṉvēytal tiruvītiyamittal mutaliya tiruppaṇikaḷum, tulāpāram iraṇiya karuppam mutaliya tāṉaṅkaḷum ceyvatu vaḻakkam.
(4) makappēṟṟu viḻā: aracaṉukkup piḷḷi piṟantapōtu koṇṭāṭappaṭum viḻā makappēṟṟu viḻāvākum. peṇ makap pēṟṟiṉum āṇ makappēṟum, āṇ makappēṟṟiṉum paṭṭattiṟ kuriya mutaṉ makaṟpēṟum ciṟappākak koṇṭāṭappeṟum.
(5) araṅkēṟṟu viḻā: muttamiḻum ōriru tamiḻumpaṟṟi nūliyaṟṟiya āciriyar,araṅkēṟṟiyatik koṇṭāṭum viḻā araṅkēṟṟu viḻāvākum.
(6) naṭukal viḻā: pōril paṭṭa ciṟanta maṟavarkkuk kalnaṭum viḻā naṭukalviḻā vākum.
(7) pattiṉi viḻā: ciṟanta pattiṉip peṇṇukku, avaḷ iṟantapiṉ kal allatu cili nāṭṭum viḻā pattiṉi viḻāvākum.
iṉi, ciṟappāvaṉa : paḵṟuḷi neṭiyōṉ eṭutta munnīr viḻāvum, mucukuntaṉum neṭumuṭikkiḷḷivarippaṭṭa avaṉ vaḻiyiṉarum koṇṭāṭiya intira viḻāvum mutalām irācarācaṉ piṟanta nāḷiṟ koṇṭāṭappaṭṭa catayat tiruviḻāvum pōṉṟavi ciṟappu viḻāvākum.
22
cila araciyaṟ karuttukaḷ
paḻantamiḻaracarum pulavarum potumakkaḷum, piṉ varumāṟu pala araciyaṟ karuttukaḷik koṇṭiruntaṉar:
 (1)	aracaṉ ulakattiṟku uyir.
 (2)	teyvam,  tāṉ, taṉ viṉiñar, kaḷvar, pakivar, vilaṅku 	ākiya aṟuvaḻiyil vanta tīṅkukaḷi aracaṉ nīkkit taṉ 	kuṭikaḷik    kāttal vēṇṭum.
 (3)	aracaṉ toḻilkaḷ, ōtal vēṭṭal ītal kāttal taṇṭittal eṉa  aintu.
 (4)	aracaveṟṟikku aṟaneṟiyāṭci kāraṇam.
 (5)	aracaveṟṟikku uḻavuttoḻil kāraṇam.
 (6) 	ceṅkōlaracaṉ nāṭṭil maḻivaḷam poḻiyum; makkaḷ oḻukkan tappār. vilaṅku ūṟu ceyyātu; kalviyum aṟamun ḻikkum. koṭuṅkōl nāṭṭil ivi nikaḻā.
 (7)	koṭuṅkōl aracaṉ āḷum nāṭṭiṉum, kaṭumpuli vāḻuṅ kāṭu naṉṟu.
 (8)	kōṉnili tiriyiṉ kōḷnili tiriyum.
 (9) 	aracaṉ iṟappik kōḷnili muṉṉaṟivikkum.
(10)	mātavar nōṉpum maṭavār kaṟpum, kāvalaṉ kāval iṉṟeṉil illi.
(11)	makkaḷi naṉṉeṟippaṭuttuvatu aracaṉ kaṭami.
(12)	maḻi peyyāmikkum viḷivuk kuṟiviṟkum iyaṟkikku māṟāṉa nikaḻccikkum, aracaṉ kāraṇam.
(13)	allaṟpaṭṭaḻuta kuṭikaḷiṉ kaṇṇīr aracaṉi aḻittuviṭum.
(14)	aṟam piḻitta aracaṉukku aṟamē kūṟṟamākum.
(15)	kuṭi paḻitūṟṟutal aracaṉukku mika iḻivu.
(16)	aracar teyvaṅkaḷi ikaḻntāl avar nāṭṭiṟku aḻivu varum.
(17)	aracarāl avamatikkappaṭṭa muṉivarum, pulavarum capikkiṉ aracum nakarum aḻiyum.
(18)	teyvaṅkaṭkup paṭikkiṉum paliyiṭiṉum maḻipeytu nāṭu ceḻikkum.
(19)	araca vāḻkki tuṉpam niṟintatu (ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ karuttu).
(20)	nāṭukaḷiṟ ciṟantatu tamiḻnāṭu (tamiḻaracar karuttu).
(21)	muttamiḻ nāṭukaḷuṭ ciṟantatu pāṇṭināṭu (pūtappāṇ ṭiyaṉ karuttu).
(22)	araciṉmiyum vēṟṟaracum nāṭṭuk kuṟṟamākum.
(23)	aracaṉ eppaṭi, kuṭikaḷ appaṭi.
(24)	aracaṉ kuṟṟam kuṭikaḷiyum kuṭikaḷ kuṟṟam aracaṉiyum cārum.
(25)	aracaṉ piṟa nāṭukaḷi velvatu kuṇamēyaṉṟik kuṟṟamaṉṟu.
(26)	oruvaṉ ceyta allatu ceyvitta kolikku avaṉi ṭattu  maṭṭumaṉṟi avaṉ kulattāriṭattum paḻi vāṅka lām. aḵtu oru teyvattiṉ kōpattit taṇippatākak karutappaṭiṉ.
(27)	koḷḷic cottum paṟimutalum kōyiṟkuc cērkkap
peṟiṉ tītilli.
(28)	pōril ceyta tīviṉikaṭkuk kōyiṟ ṟiruppaṇikaḷ kaḻuvāy.
(29)	cittarum, muṉivarum pōṉṟa periyārit tuṇik kōṭal aracark karaṇceyyum; avarip piḻittal avarukku aḻivu tarum.
23
nampaṟkariya ceytikaḷ
paḻantamiḻāṭciyil nikaḻntaṉavākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa pala ceytikaḷ, ikkālattāl nampaṟkariyaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cila varumāṟu:
(1)	kaḻumalattil aviḻttuviṭṭa yāṉi, karikālaṉit tēṭic ceṉṟu karuvūriṟ kaṇṭu tāṉē taṉmītu ēṟṟik koṇṭu vantatu.
(2)	ūrkkaṇakkaṉ āṇṭiṟutiyil kāycciya, irumpēntik kaṇakkoppuvittatu.
(3)	karikālaṉ mukariyeṉṉum mukkaṇṇaṉiṉ mikik kaṇṇip paṭattilaḻikka, an noṭiyē am mukariyiṉ kaṇ tāṉē maṟintatu.
(4)	pāmpukkuṭamum,paḻukkak kāycciya irumpum, aṟamaṉṟaṅ kaḷil teyvaccāṉṟāka uṇmiyuṇartti vantatu.
(5)	pukāriluḷḷa catukkappūtam, aṟipōkamiccarum poyk kariyāḷarum uḷḷiṭṭa aṟuvakik kuṟṟavāḷi kaḷip   puṭittuṇṭatu.
(6)	vārttikaṉi neṭuñceḻiyaṉ atikārikaḷ ciṟiyiliṭṭa vuṭaṉ, maturik kāḷikōyil tāṉē cāttikkoṇṭatu.
(7)	mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkaḷ oru pirāmaṇaṉukkuk kolit taṇṭaṉi vitikka, at taṇṭaṉi niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭa piṉ, ap pirāmaṇaṉ āvi iravum pakalum aracaṉit toṭarntu tuṉpuṟutti, iṟutiyil tiruviṭimarutūril nīṅkiṉatu.
24
aṉṟāṭa nikaḻcci
aracar vāḻkkiyil aṉṟāṭa nikaḻccikaḷ potuvākap piṉvarumāṟu nikaḻntuvantaṉa:
vikaṟiyil, akavar (cūtar) aracaṉiṉ paḷḷi māṭattiṟku veḷiyē vāyilaruku niṉṟu, avaṉ muṉṉōriṉ pukaḻi iṉitākap pāṭi avaṉit tuyiluṇarttuvar.
acaraṉ tuyiluṇarntu, aṉṟu ceyyavēṇṭiyavaṟṟic cintittup pularum vēḷiyiṟ paḷḷiviṭṭeḻuvaṉ. avaṉ eḻun tamikkaṟikuṟiyākak kāli muracam eṉṉum paḷḷiyeḻucci muracam aṭikkappeṟum.
aracaṉ eḻuntapiṉ, paṭikkalap payiṟciyiruppiṉ atic ceytuviṭṭuk kālikkaṭaṉkaḷi muṭittu nīrāṭi iṟivaḻipāṭu muṟṟik kāliyuṇavuṇṭu, ōlakka maṇṭapattiṟkuc celvaṉ.
aṅkuk koṭimuracu aṭikkappaṭum. palvēṟiṭattiṉiṉ ṟum, vantirukkum, pulavar pāṇar kūttar mutaliya paricilar, pāṭiyum āṭiyum tattam tiṟamikāṭṭi, ataṟkut taka aracaṉiṭam paricu peṟuvar.
koṭi nikaḻtti muṭintavuṭaṉ, muṟimuracu aṭikkappaṭum. vaḻakkāḷikaḷ tattam vaḻakkic colvataṟku muṉṉarē vantiruppar.  muracaṭittavuṭaṉ vaḻakkuk kēṭki toṭaṅkum.
aracaṉ ōlakkamaṇṭapattiliruntu koṭi nikaḻtti muṭinta piṉ, avvak kāla viṉinilimik kēṟpac cūḻviṉi maṇṭapat tiṟkēṉum aṟamaṉṟattiṟkēṉum celliṉum cellalām; aṉṟi ōlakka maṇṭapattilēyē iruntu taṉṉāṭ ṭeḻuttup pōkku varattiyum ayalnāṭṭeḻuttup pōkkuvarattiyum kavaṉip piṉum kavaṉikkalām.
iv viṉikaḷellām muṟpakal nikaḻccikaḷākum.
naṇpakalil aracaṉ uṇavuṇṭapiṉ iraṇṭoru nāḻiki allatu veyil taṇiyumvari iḷippāṟalām. ataṉpiṉ mīṇṭum ōlakkamaṇṭapam  ceṉṟu ciṟitu nēram araciyaṟ kāriyaṅkaḷik kavaṉikkalām.
piṟpakal nikaḻccikaḷ potuvāka, aracarkkuk kuṭumpak kāriyak kavaṉippākavum, iṉpap poḻutupōkkākavumē yirukkum.
irāvuṇavu uṇṭapiṉ, ciṟitunēram uriyāṭiyiruntu, ūraṭaṅkum vēḷiyil aracaṉ paḷḷikoḷvaṉ. paḷḷimāṭat tiṟkup paḷḷimaṇṭapam, paḷḷiyampalam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu.
kārkālattilum, pōrkkālattilum, viḻānāḷilum nāṭu kāvaṟ cuṟṟuppōkku nāḷkaḷilum, piṟa ciṟappu viṉinikaḻcci yiṉpōtum, mēṟkūṟiya aṉṟāṭa nikaḻcci viṉikaḷ māṟiyum nikaḻum.
potuvāka, aṟatti uyirnāṭiyākak koṇṭirunta ceṅkōlaracarellām, evviṭattum ennērattum, perumuṟi kēṭāṉa kāriyaṅkaḷik kavaṉittaṟku āyattamāyiruntatākavē terikiṉṟatu.
"talimakaṉāl taliniṉṟu oḻukappaṭuvaṉa tarumam aruttam kāmam eṉa mūṉṟu; am mūṉṟiṉiyum oru pakali mūṉṟu kūṟiṭṭu mutaṟkaṭ pattu nāḻikiyum aṟattoṭu paṭaccellum, iṭiyaṉa pattu nāḻikiyum, aruttattoṭu paṭaccellum; kaṭiyaṉa pattu nāḻikiyum kāmattoṭu paṭac cellum; ātalāṉ talimakaṉ mutaṟkaṇ pattunāḻiki tarumattoṭu paṭuvāṉ; talimakaḷum vēṇṭavē tāṉum vēṇṭip pōntu attāṇi pukuntu aṟaṅ kēṭpatum aṟattoṭu paṭac colvatuñ ceyyum, nāḻiki aḷantukoṇṭu iṭiyaṉa pattu nāḻikiyum iṟiyum muṟiyuṅ kēṭṭu aruttattiṉoṭu paṭṭaṉavē ceytu vāḻvāṉām. av varuttattu nīkkattuk kaṭiyaṉa pattu nāḻikiyil talimakaḷuḻip pōtarum," eṉpatu iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷuri  (pa. 226).
aracarkkuk kālikkaṭaṉ kaḻittalum, ulakattiliruntu nāṭu kāvaṟṟoḻil celuttalum, viruntuṭaṉ aṭicil kitoṭa lum, nāvalaroṭu kalvi payiṟalum, āṭal pāṭalkaḷiṟ kaḷittalum, maṭavāroṭu kūṭalum, tuyiṟalum, tuyiluṇarntu tēvarp parāvalu mākiya kālavari eṭṭu, eṉpatu tañcivāṇaṉ kōviyuri (pa. 248).
25
illaṟa vāḻkki
mūvēntarum, perunila maṉṉar kuṭiyilum kuṟunila maṉṉar kuṭiyilum vēḷir kuṭiyilum peṇ koṇṭaṉar. avarukkup pala tēviyar iruntaṉar. avaruḷ taliyāṉ kōpperuntēvikku aracaṉukkuḷḷa matippiruntatu. ōlakka maṇṭapattilum cūḻviṉik kūṭṭattilum nakarvalattilum, kōpperuntēviyum aracaṉoṭu kūṭa amarntiruppatu vaḻakkam.
tēvimāruḷ aracaṉāl ciṟappāka kātalikkappaṭupavaḷ kāmakkiḻatti eṉappaṭuvāḷ. oruvarkkē urimi pūṇuṅ kulap parattimakaḷāyk kāmaṅkāraṇamākat talimakaṉāl varintu koḷḷappaṭṭavaḷ kāmakkiḻatti eṉa, nampiyakap poruḷ kūṟum. utayakumaraṉ maṇimēkaliyi ivvakiyil maṇakka virumpiyiruk kalām.
caṅkakālat taracar cilar cērip parattiyaroṭum toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. parattiyar cēriyiluḷḷa makaḷirkkuc cēripparattiyar eṉṟum, avaruḷ aracaṉāl kātalikkappaṭupavaṭkuk kātaṟ paratti yeṉṟum, avaṉāl varintukoḷḷappaṭṭa parattikku iṟparatti yeṉṟum peyar. aracaṉ toṭarpukoḷḷum parattiyarellām, oruvarkkē yurimi pūṇuṅ kulap parattiyārākavē yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
aracaṉāṟ kātalikkappaṭum parattiyarellām, avaṉu rimiyeṉa avaṉ peṟṟōrālēyē otukkappaṭṭu iḷamimutal vaḷarkkappaṭṭavar eṉṟu, iṟiyaṉā rakapporuḷuriyāciriyar kūṟuvar (pak. 226-pa.8.)
iṉi, aracaṉ vēṭṭikkum ulāviṟkum ceṉṟaviṭattu yārēṉum ōraḻakiya aracakaṉṉiyit taṉiyiṭattuk kāṇiṉ, avaḷik kaḷavumaṇam purivatumuṇṭu. neṭumuṭikkiḷḷi nāka nāṭṭaracaṉ makaḷākiya pīlivaḷiyik kaṭaṟkariyiṟ kaṇṭu kaḷavumaṇam  purintatāka, maṇimēkali kūṟum. iyalumiṭamel lām kaḷavu kaṟpākat toṭarum. aracaṉ maṇattiṟku ittuṇi yeṉa oru varampillātiruntatu.
araṇmaṉiyil makaḷir uṟivataṟkut taṉiyiṭamuṇṭu. aḵtu uvaḷakam eṉappaṭum. atil aracaṉiyum iḷavaracariyun tavirap paruvamaṭinta āṭavar vēṟu yārum pukuvatilli. kuṟaḷaruñ cintarum peṇṭirum cōṉakap pēṭiyarum vāḷēnti uvaḷakattik kākkumāṟu amarttappaṭṭiruntaṉar. kaṉṉiyar uṟivataṟkut taṉimāṭam iruntatu. atu kaṉṉikimāṭam eṉappaṭṭatu. aracaṉ uvaḷakañ cellum pōtellām makaḷir parivāramē uṭaṉ cellum. tēviyarukkut taṉittaṉi māḷiki yiruntatu.
aracaṉ vēṟoruttiyik kātalittāṉ eṉṟō vēṟu kāraṇampaṟṟiyō, kōpperuntēvi ūṭiyirukkumpōtu, pulavar pāṇar mutaliyōr vāyilāy avaḷ ūṭalit tīrppatu aracaṉ vaḻakkam.
aracaṉukkum aracikkum, aṭimiyarum tōḻiyarum kuṟṟēva larum parivārattārum, meykāvalarumākap paṟpala ūḻiyar iruntaṉar. araci cellumiṭamellām parivāra makaḷir eṇ maṅkala (aṭṭamaṅkala)p poruḷkaḷi ēntic celluvatu vaḻakkam.
nakiccuvi yuṇarccimikka aracar, tam conta iṉpattiṟ keṉṟu oru nakiyāṇṭiyi amarttikkoḷvatu muṇṭu. avaṉukkuk kōmāḷi allatu kōṇaṅki eṉṟu peyar.
araṇmaṉi vēlikkeṉṟu taṉiyāka, aivakik kammi yarum, mayirviṉiñaṉ vaṇṇāṉ cemmāṉ mutaliya kuṭimak kaḷum, piṟa toḻilāḷarum amarttappeṟṟiruntaṉar. avari araṇmaṉit taṭṭāṉ araṇmaṉi mayirviṉiñaṉ araṇ maṉik kuyavaṉ eṉa aḻippatu vaḻakkam. araṇmaṉip poṟ kollaṉ caṭṭiyaṇintiruntāṉ eṉṟu cilappatikāraṅ kūṟuva tāl, aracaṉoṭu neruṅkip paḻakupavarkkellām ac ciṟappaḷik kappaṭṭiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
aracaṉukku uṭpakiyum aṟipōku viṉiñarumpaṟṟiya accamirukkumpōtu, araṇmaṉi maruttuvar avaṉ uṇavi aṉṉam cakakaravākam karuṅkuraṅku mutaliya uyirkaḷikkoṇṭu nōṭṭañ ceyvar. aracaṉ uṇṭapiṉ avaṉukku veṟṟili maṭittuk koṭuppavaṉukku aṭippiyāṉ (aṭippik kāraṉ) eṉṟu peyar.
araṇmaṉic celavellām araciyaṟ celavākavē karutap paṭṭatu. araciyaṟ celavum araṇmaṉic celavum pōka eñciya potuvaki (cātāraṇa)p poruḷkaḷellām, koṭikkup payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. aracarkkē takum aruviliyaṇikalaṉ kaḷum, perumaṇikaḷum, peruntokiyāṉa poṟkācu kaḷum, aracaṉ conta uṭimiyākap pōṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. kuṟippiṭṭa niṟikku mēṟpaṭṭa maṇikkaṟkaḷik kuṭikaḷ vittirukkavāvatu nāṭu kaṭattavāvatu kūṭāteṉṟum, aracaṉiṭattil oppuvittu vilipeṟṟukkoḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟum kaṇṭippāṉa aracaviti yiruntatu. aracaṉukkuc contamāka irunta nilattiṟkuk kaṇṭuḻavu eṉṟu peyar. araciyarkkuc ciṟupāṭṭuc celaviṟkeṉṟu nilaṅkaḷum ūrkaḷum viṭappaṭṭu iruntatākavum terikiṉṟatu.
aracaṉ iṉpamākap poḻutupōkkuvataṟkup pala vaḻikaḷ iruntaṉa. kaṇpōlum naṇparōṭu aḷavaḷāvalum, ōṅkiya ciṟappiṉ uyarnta kēḷvip pulavaroṭu payilalum, nakiyāṇṭi coṟkēṭṭalum, vaṭṭāṭṭu, vallāṭṭu, puṉalāṭṭu mutaliya viḷiyāṭṭukaḷi yāṭalum ilavantikic cōlikkum ceykuṉṟiṟkum neytalaṅkāṉalukkum ulāp pōtalum, pāṇar pāṭiṉiyar kaṇikiyariṉ ici kēṭṭalum, viṟaliyar kaṇikiyar cākkiyariṉ kūttuk kāṇṭalum, vēṭṭiyāṭalum, malivaḷaṅ kāṇalum, piṟavum aracaṉ iṉpamākap poḻutupōkkum vaḻikaḷām. aracaṉ ulāppōkum pōtu tōḻiyaroṭu celvatu vaḻakkam.1  avarukku aracaṉoṭu meytoṭṭup payilavum viḷiyāṭavum urimiyuṇṭu.
viyamuñ civikiyu maṇikkā lamaḷiyum
 uyyā ṉatti ṉuṟutuṇi makiḻcciyuñ
 cāmarik kavariyun tamaṉiya vaṭippiyuṅ
 kūrnuṉi vāḷuṅ kōmakaṉ koṭuppap
 peṟṟa celvam piṟaḻā vāḻkkip
 poṟṟoṭi maṭantiyar	               (14.126-131)
eṉṉum cilappatikārap pakutiyāl aracaṉ parattiyaroṭu ulāppōtalum, avarkku avaṉ paṟpala variciyaḷittalum, uṇṭeṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
aracar, paruvamvanta putalvarit tuṇiyariyarāka allatu maṇṭalat talivarāka, vevvēṟiṭat tamartti vippatu perum
pāṉmi. avar tam putalviyari araca kuṭiyiṉarkkaṉṟip piṟarkkuk koṭuppatilliyeṉpatu, 3ām kulōttuṅkaṉ ampikā patiyik koṉṟatiṉāl aṟiyappaṭum.
nāṭukāval, cantu (pakitaṇiviṉi), pōr, naṭparacar, viḻā mutaliya kāraṇampaṟṟi, aracaṉ taṉ tēviyariṭattiṉiṉṟu pirintupōvaṉ, pirivuk kālattil, iṉṟiyamiyum aṇikaḻaṟṟa lum, meyyalaṅkariyāmiyum, aḷavāka vuṇṭalum, nāḷeṇ ṇik kaḻittalum, makiḻātiruttalum. teyvatti vēṇṭalum, mīṭcik kālattil varavukaṇṭu makiḻtalum viruntayartalum tēviyar mēṟkoḷḷuñ ceyalkaḷām.
aracaṉ iṟappiṉ, tēviyarum uṭaṉ iṟappar; allatu uṭaṉ kaṭṭiyēṟuvar; allatu kimmi pūṇuvar. avar muṉṉiruvakiyil iṟappatē perumpāṉmi.
26
aracar muṭivu
tamiḻaracar muṭivu taṉippaṭṭatum kuṭippaṭṭatum eṉa iruvaki.
aracar taṉimuṭivu: tamiḻaracar, pōril iṟattaliyē, perum pēṟākavum,viṇṇulakam pukum vaḻiyākavuṅ koṇṭiruntaṉar. ataṉāl, iyalumpōtellām avarum cēṉiyuṭaṉ pōrukkuc celluvatu vaḻakkam. pōrilaṉṟi nōyālum mūppālum iṟakkum aracar vāḷpōḻntaṭakkap peṟuvar. ataṉ iyalpi,
nōyppāl viḷinta yākki taḻīik
 kātaṉ maṟantavar tītumaruṅ kaṟumār
 aṟampuri koḷki nāṉmaṟi mutalvar
 tiṟampuri pacumpuṟ parappiṉar kiṭappi
 maṟaṅkan tāka nallamar vīḻnta
 nīḷkaḻaṉ maṟavar celvuḻic celkeṉa
 vāḷpōḻn taṭakkal		(puṟam. 93: 5-11)
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭup pakutiyāl aṟika. caṅkak kālattiṟkup piṟkālattil, vāḷpōḻntaṭakkal viṭappaṭṭatu.
pōriṟ peṟṟa puṟappuṇ nāṇiyum, putalvar taṉṉoṭu poravaruki nāṇiyum, piṟavakiyil māṉakkēṭu nērntaviṭattum, ulakappaṟṟit tuṟantaviṭattum, aracar ūrukku vaṭakkil uṇṇānōṉpiruntu uyir tuṟappar. atu vaṭakkiruttal eṉappaṭum. pakivarāl ciṟippaṭṭiruntu uṇṇātiṟattalum vaṭakkiruttaliṉ pāṟpaṭum.
vāḻnāḷiṟutiyil, aracāṭciyi iḷavaracaṉiṭam oppuvittu viṭṭuk kāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu tavañceytal, orōvōr aracaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa ceyalākum.
aracarkkup piṟappiṉum, iṟappē ciṟappātalāl, koṟṟa aṭimoḻiyillāta aracarellām, avar iṟanta iṭappeya rāl vēṟupaṭuttik kūṟappaṭṭaṉar.
aracariṟantapiṉ, avaruṭampit tāḻiyāṟ kavittalum, tāḻiyāṟ kavittup putittalum, veṟumaṉē putittalum, muṟkāla vaḻakkam; erittal piṟkāla vaḻakkam. aracarukku iṟutic caṭaṅkiyaṟṟal paḷḷipaṭuttal (paḷḷipaṭi) eṉṟum, avar niṉivukkuṟiyāka eḻuppappaṭuṅ kaṭṭaṭam paḷḷippaṭik kōyil eṉṟum peyarpeṟum.
aracakkuṭi muṭivu: muttamiḻaracarum tamiḻālum maṇavuṟa vālum iṇikkappaṭṭu oṟṟumiyāyirunta vari, acōkap pēraracaṉālum, avari acikka muṭiyavilli. caṅkakālattiṟkup piṟkālattil, tamiḻ pēṇāmi oṟṟumiyiṉmi kulappiriviṉi mutaliya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl, muttamiḻ nāṭum mūvēṟu pirintu valimi kuṉṟippōyc cōḻapāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷ mutalāvatu 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tilli makamatiyap paṭikaṭkum piṉpu 16ām   nūṟṟāṇṭil vicayanakara vaṭukap paṭikaṭkum eḷitāy iriyāyiṉa. pāṇṭināṭṭu valimi ataṉ tāyap pōrkaḷālum tēyntu māyntatu. iṟutippāṇṭiyaṉ ki. pi. 1652 vari iruntāṉ.
cērar kuṭiyil iṟutiyāka virunta aracar valimi yaṟṟavarā yiruntatiṉāl, cēranāṭāṉatu. vēṇāṭu (tiruvitaṅkōṭu)kocci kōḻikkōṭu kōlattunāṭu mutaliya pala ciṟu taṉiyaracukaḷākap pirintupōṉatuṭaṉ, 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭacoṟ kalappāl maliyāḷa nāṭākavum tirintuviṭṭatu.
iṅṅaṉam, paṭippuk kālantoṭṭu mēmpaṭṭu vantavi yeṉap pārāṭṭappeṟṟa mūvēntar kuṭikaḷum, 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭi ṭiyil muṭivuṟṟaṉa.
piṉṉiṇippu
1. makkaḷ nilimi
  tamiḻaracar kālattil,  tamiḻ makkaḷ, tiṇinili tiṇi mayakkanili ākiya irunilippaṭṭu vāḻntu vantaṉar. kuṟiñciyil kuṟavarum mulliyil iṭiyarum marutattil uḻavarum neytalil cempaṭavarum pāliyil kaḷḷar maṟavar mutaliyōrumāka ovvoru tiṇik kulamum taṉittaṉi vāḻvatu tiṇinili; ciṟṟūrilum pērurilum nakarilum mānakarilum pala tiṇikkulam kalantu vāḻvatu tiṇi mayakka nili.
mutalāvatu marutanilattilum piṉṉarp piṟa nilattūrkaḷilum tiṇimayakka niliyālum toḻiṟpiriviṉā lum pala kulaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭiruntaṉa.  āciriyarum pūcāriyārum tuṟaviyarum antaṇar eṉṟum āṭciviṉi pūṇṭōr  aracar eṉṟum, viṟpaṉiyum iruvaki vāṉikamum mēṟkoṇṭōr vaṇikar eṉṟum, uḻutum uḻuvittum uṇpōr vēḷāḷar  eṉṟum, kittoḻil ceypavar viṉivalar eṉṟum, kuṟṟēval ceypavar ēvalar eṉṟum, irantuṇpōr irappōr eṉṟum marutanili makkaḷ eḻuperu vakuppiṉarāka vakukkappaṭṭu nālvaki nilattum nāṭu muḻutum paraviṉar.
muṟkūṟiyavāṟu perunakaraṅkaḷil vantu taṅkiya ayal nāṭṭār,  nāṭupaṟṟiya kulattiṉarāy vāḻntu vantaṉar.
makkaḷellārum perumpālum muṉṉōr toḻiliyē ceytu vantālum, avarukku virumpiya toḻili mēṟkoḷḷa urimi yiruntatu.  piṟappuppaṟṟiya kulappirivum, ap pirivupaṟṟiya ēṟṟat tāḻvum, tīṇṭāmiyum muṟkālattililli.  toḻil paṟṟiya kulappirivum, aṟivum tuppuravum oḻukkamumpaṟṟiya ēṟṟattāḻvumē iruntuvantaṉa.  kalvi ellārukkum potu vāyiruntatu.  pāṇar ici nāṭakat toḻiliyum, vaḷḷuvara kaṇiyat toḻiliyum maḻiviṉiñar  celliyat toḻiliyum (Surgery), eṅkum naṭatti vantaṉar.
karumpu tiṉṉak kikkūli  koṭukkavum, kuḻikoṇṭu kōḻi yeṟiyavum viḷivuñ celvamum viñciyiruntaṉa.
aṟivum aruḷuñcāṉṟa antaṇarum tammiyun tam putalvariyum muṟiceyyum aracarum,
vaṭuvañci vāymoḻintu
 tamavum piṟavum oppa nāṭik
koḷvatūu mikikoḷātu koṭuppatū uṅ kuṟi koṭātu
palpaṇṭam pakarntuvīcum1 		(paṭṭiṉappāli, 208-211)
vaṇikarum, viruntirukka vuṇṇāta vēḷāḷarum palki yiruntaṉar.
poruḷātāratti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu uḻavaṉiyē talimik kuṭivāṇaṉākap paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭu pōṟṟivantatu.  makkaḷiṭi oṟṟumiyum aracaṉukkum kuṭikaṭkum iṭiyē irutaliyaṉpum iruntuvantaṉa.  aracaṉāṇi eṉṟa toṭar moḻikkuk kūṭa aṉivarum kaṭṭuppaṭṭiruntaṉar.
piṟappuppaṟṟiya kulappirivu tamiḻnāṭṭil ēṟpapaṭṭati liruntu,  tamiḻnāṭṭu nilimāṟiviṭṭatu, tamiḻarukkuḷ kulappaṟṟu mikuntu iṉavuṇarcci (National feeling) kuṉṟippōyiṟṟu.  tīṇṭāmiyālum atiṉiluṅ koṭiya kāṇāmiyālum tākkuṇṭa orucārt tamiḻiṉam taṉ cīriya nilimiyiyum, makkaḷurimiyiyum iḻantatu.  curuṅkac colliṉ, tamiḻiṉam muḻutum palvēṟuvakiyil ciṉṉa piṉṉappaṭṭut tāḻvaṭintu viṭṭatu.
aṭikkaṭi nikaḻnta pōrālum, iṭiyum iṟutiyum ēṟpaṭṭa ayalār āṭciyālum, paṭiyēṟu kuḻappattālum. koḷḷiyālum, veḷḷattālum, pañcattālum, varik koṭumiyālum, potumakkaḷ nilimi varavarac cīr keṭṭu vantatu.  vaṟumiyum aṭimittaṉamum vaḷarntōṅkiṉa.  taṉiyaṭi miyum kottaṭimiyum kulavaṭimiyumākap paṟpalar mīḷā aṭimippaṭṭu, avar vaḻiyiṉarum an niliyarāyiṉar.  veḷiyār tiraḷtiraḷāy vantu kuvintatiṉālum, makkaḷ toki mikkatiṉā lum, poruḷātāra vītaṅ kuṉṟiṟṟu.  kuṭikaḷ valaci pōṉatiṉāl cila ūrkaḷ kuṭippāḻāyiṉa.  aṉpāṭci nīṅki atikāravāṭci ōṅkiyatāl, kuṭikaḷ perumpālum añci vāḻa vēṇṭiyatā yiṟṟu.  makkaḷiṭiyē oṟṟumiyum nāṭṭuppaṟṟum aracap paṟṟum maṟintaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam, tamiḻāṭci muṭivuṟṟa 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭut tamiḻakam, palavakiyilum alippuṇṭu allaṟpaṭṭiruntatu.
2.moḻi nilimi
mutaṟkālattil, tamiḻ ellāt tuṟikaḷilum taṉiyāṭci celutti muḻuttūymiyāy vaḻaṅki vantatu.  āyiṉ, iṭik kālattil vaṭamoḻi tēvapāṭi eṉṟu mūvēntarālum oppuk koḷḷappaṭṭatiṉāl purōkitar naṭattum pūci karaṇaṅkaṭ kellām tamiḻ vilakkappaṭṭatu.  vaṭacol vaḻaṅkuvatu uyarvu eṉṉuṅ karuttum nāḷaṭivil eḻuntatu.  tamiḻaracarum orucila tamiḻppulavarum tamiḻip puṟakkaṇikkavun talippaṭṭaṉar.
toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻccaṅkam irīit tamiḻi vaḷarttut tamiḻnāṭaṉ  eṉṟu  ciṟappikkap peṟṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ kuṭiyiṉarē, ki.pi. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭil caṅkattik kulittuviṭṭaṉar.  ataṉ piṉ, 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil atip putuppikkumāṟu poyyā moḻip pulavar ceyta perumuyaṟciyum vīṇāyppōyiṟṟu.
vaṭacoṟkaḷ tolkāppiyar kālattilēyē, tamiḻil iṭampeṟat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉa.  āyiṉ, avar kālattil taṟpava mākavum citivākavum orōvoṉṟāy arukiyē vaḻaṅkiṉa.  piṟkālattilō, avi paṭippaṭiyāyp peruki vantatuṭaṉ, kaṭic caṅkattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa kālattil, potumakkaṭ keṉṟēṟpaṭṭa kalveṭṭukaḷil taṟcamamākavum kirantaveḻutti lum tōṉṟi, kalveṭṭut tamiḻiyē tamiḻarkku viḷaṅkātatum piḻi malintatumāṉa maṇippavaḻak kalavi moḻivaḻiyāka māṟṟiviṭṭaṉa. aracar peyar1 teyvap peyar iṭappeyar mutaliya palvakip peyarkaḷum nāḷaṭivil vaṭacoṟkaḷāy māṟiṉa.
eṭuttukkāṭṭu:

kiyeḻuttu,  fšbt£L.
(taṭitta eḻuttuḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷ avaṟṟukku nērāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppākum)
iṉi, cila teṉcoṟkaḷ tam teṉmoḻi vaṭiviḻantu vaṭamoḻi vaṭivil vaḻaṅkat talippaṭṭaṉa.
eṭuttukkāṭṭu:
teṉmoḻi	vaṭivam          	vaṭamoḻi vaṭivam
aracaṉ                          	rājaṉ
avi                            	capi
tiru                              	srī
paṭi                              	pirati
makkaḷit tākkiya kulappiriviṉi nāḷaṭivil ilakkiyattiyum tākkiṟṟu.  veṇpā antaṇarpā eṉṟum, āciriyappā aracarpā eṉṟum, kalippā vaṇikarpā eṉṟum, vañcippā vēḷāḷarpā eṉṟum, vakukkappaṭṭatuṭaṉ, kalampakac ceyyuḷkaḷ tēvarkku nūṟum, antaṇarkkut toṇṇūṟṟin tum aracarkkut toṇṇūṟum, amiccarkku eḻupatum, vaṇikarkku aimpatum, vēḷāḷarkkum muppatum pāṭappaṭum eṉavum ilakkaṇam vitikkappaṭṭatu. pāṇarit tīṇṭāmi tākkiyatāl, icittamiḻum nāṭakat tamiḻum 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkumēl vaḻakkoḻintaṉa.  avaṟṟiṟkuriya nūlkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻ kaṟpārkkup pōtiya araciyal ūkkuvippiṉmiyāl, mutaliru caṅkakālattum muttamiḻāyirunta ilakkaṇanūl, kaṭic caṅka kālattil oru tamiḻāyum, 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkumēl atuvum niṟiviṉṟi eḻuttuc collaḷavāyum kuṟukiviṭṭatu.
tamiḻ vaṭamoḻi kiḷiyeṉak karutappaṭṭu, 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇan taḻuvi vīracōḻiyam eṉṉum tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlum iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu.
kaṭiccaṅka kālattiliruntu āriyak karuttukaḷum tamiḻi lakkiyattiṟ pukuttappaṭṭatiṉāl, tamiḻ varalāṟṟilum tamiḻ nāṭṭu varalāṟṟilum purāṇak karuttukaḷ pukuntaṉa.  piṟkālattu nūlkaḷellām perumpālum kalittuṟi paṟṟātu matattuṟiyē paṟṟiṉa.
14ām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāṭappaṭṭa villipāratattil vaṭa coṟkaḷ varituṟiyiṉṟi āḷappaṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻppulavariyuṅ kaliñariyum pōṟṟuvāriṉmiyāl, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tamiḻnūlkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.  tūyatamiḻ tāḻttap paṭṭōr moḻi eṉat tāḻttappaṭṭatiṉāl nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷum maṟintoḻintaṉa.                 
3.cīmiyarāṭci vakikaḷ
cīmi eṉpatu,
(1)   nakarac cīmi (City State)
(2)   iṉac cīmi (Nation state)
(3)   tēyac cīmi (Country state)
(4)   kūṭṭuccīmi (Federal State)
eṉa nālvakiyākak kūṟappaṭum. orē pērūrāyiruppatu, allatu orē nakaramum ataṉic cārnta ciṟṟūrkaḷum cērntatu nakaraccīmi;  orēyiṉa makkaḷ vāḻum nāṭṭuc cīmi iṉaccīmi; pala iṉamakkaḷ vāḻum  nāṭṭuc cīmi tēyac cīmi; pala nāṭukaḷ cērntatu kūṭṭuc cīmi.
cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷ mūṉṟum ōriṉa makkaḷiyē koṇṭaṉavāyum, vevvēṟu araciṉkīḻppaṭṭaṉavāyum irunta tiṉāl, avaṟṟi āṅkila nilaccīmi (Territorial State) eṉa aḻikkalām.
akapporuṭ ceyyuṭ kiḷavittalivar, veṟpaṉ kuṟum poṟināṭaṉ ūraṉ tuṟivaṉ viṭali eṉak kuṟunilat talivarākavum taṉiyūrt talivarākavumē kuṟikkap peṟuvatāl, cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyavaracukaḷ tōṉṟumuṉ nakarac cīmikaḷē tamiḻakatti liruntaṉa eṉak koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu.
aracu allatu araciyal eṉpatu,
(1) nfhtuR(Monarchy)
(2) kuṭiyaracu (Democracy)
(3) ÓÇnahuuR(Aristrocracy)
eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
kōvaraci muṭiyaracu eṉṟuṅ kūṟalām.  muttamiḻaracuṅ kōvaracākum.  piṟkālattil pārppaṉac celvākku mikkiruntati ṉāl, ati oruvakip pārppaṉac cīriyōraracu (Brahmin Aristocracy) eṉṉalām.
tamiḻkkōvaracu perumpālum ceṅkōlāṭciyākavum (Righteous Government) ciṟupāṉmi koṭuṅkōlāṭciyākavum (Despotic Government) iruntatu. iraṇṭum muṟṟatikārak kōvaracē (Absolute Monarchy). nilitta āṭci (Stable Government) poṟuppāṭci (Responsible Government) eṉṉun taṉmikaḷ ceṅkōlāṭcik kiruntaṉa.
ciṟṟaracar nāṭukaṭkellām taṉṉāṭci (Autonomy) vaḻaṅkappaṭṭiruntatu.
iṟṟi araciyal nūl (Politics) vakukkum cīmip pākupāṭ ṭiṉpaṭi, tamiḻc cīmikaḷiṉ vakimiyik kūṟiṉ, nekiḻum caṭṭamuṟimiyum (Flexible Constitution) tērtal peṟāk karumac cuṟṟamum (Non-elective Executive), caṭṭa nītiyum (Rule of Law), koṇṭa tārāḷat (Liberal) taṉic cīmi (Unitary State) eṉṉalām.  piṟkālattil āriyak kulappiriviṉiyāl ēṟpaṭṭa kulavāri nītiyi, āḷviṉi nīti (Administrative Law) eṉṉalām 
4. vēttiyal eḻuttum nūlkaḷum
eḻutappaṭṭa aracavāṇikkut tiruvōli, tirumukam nīṭṭu, āṇatti eṉap pala peyaruṇṭu. aracaṉ kiyeḻuttiṟkut tiruveḻuttu eṉṟum, avaṉ kiyeḻuttiṭutaṟkut tiruveḻuttuc cāttutal eṉṟum peyar.
aracaṉiyum, aracaṉuṭiya uṟuppukaḷ ciṉṉaṅkaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟiyum, pukaḻntu kūṟum eḻuttīṭum ceyyuḷum nūlkaḷum varumāṟu:-
 (1)	nāṇmaṅkalam : aracaṉukku ittuṇi yāṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟaṉa eṉṟeḻutuvatum, ceṅkōlaracaṉiṉ piṟanta nāḷic ciṟappittuk kūṟuvatum, nāṇmaṅkalam eṉappaṭum.
 (2)	vaḷamaṭal : peṇṇiṉpamē ciṟantateṉak koṇṭu, aracaṉiṉ iyaṟpeyarkkut takka etuki varak kaliveṇpāvāṟ pāṭuvatu vaḷamaṭal.
 (3)	ulā: aracaṉ tērmaṟukil veṟṟiyulā varakkaṇṭa eḻuparuva iḷamakaḷirum, avaṉik kātalittatākak kaliveṇ pāvāṟ pāṭuvatu ulāvākum.   
(4) meykkīrtti : aracaṉuṭiya meykkīrttic ceyalkaḷi akavalāṟ pāṭuvatu meykkīrtti.
(5) meykkīrtti māli: aracaṉuṭiya meykkīrt tiyippaṟṟiya pala ceyyuḷkaḷ pāṭuvatu  meykkīrtti māli.
(6) tāṉi māli: koṭippaṭiyi akavalāṟ pāṭuvatu tāṉi māli.
(7) varalāṟṟu vañci: aracaṉ paṭiyeṭuttuc cel vatip pāṭuvatu varalāṟṟu vañci.
(8) cerukkaḷa vañci: cerukkaḷattil nikaḻnta pōrip paṟṟip pāṭuvatu cerukkaḷavañci.
iṅṅaṉam piṟa puṟattiṇikaḷippaṟṟip pāṭuvaṉavum avva tiṇiyāṟ peyarpeṟum.
(9) kaḷavaḻi vāḻttu: aracaṉ pōrkkaḷattiṟ peṟṟa celvattic ciṟappittup pāṭuvatu kaṇavaḻi vāḻttu.
(10) paraṇi : kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu,kaṭitiṟappu, kāṭu pāṭal, kōyil pāṭal, tēviyip pāṭal, pēykaḷip pāṭal, intiracālam, irācapārampariyam, pēy muṟippāṭu. avatāram, kāḷikkuk kūḷi kūṟal, kaḷaṅkāṭṭal, kūḻaṭutal eṉṉum patiṉmūṉṟuṟuppamiya, ōr aracaṉiṉ pōrveṟṟiyip palvaki yīraṭit tāḻiciyāṟ pāṭuvatu paraṇiyākum.
(11) peyariṉṉici : aracaṉiṉ peyaric ciṟappittu, toṇṇūṟu allatu eḻupatu allatu aimpatu iṉṉici veṇpāp pāṭuvatu peyariṉṉici.
(12) ūriṉṉici: aracaṉiṉ talikaric ciṟappittu, mēṟkūṟiyapaṭi pāṭuvatu ūriṉṉici.
(13) peyarnērici: aracaṉiṉ peyaric ciṟappittu, mēṟkūṟiya aḷavu nērici veṇpāp pāṭuvatu peyar nērici.
(14) ūr nērici: aracaṉiṉ talinakaric ciṟappittu mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu pāṭuvatu ūr nērici.
(15) tacāṅkappattu: aracaṉuṭiya mali, āṟu, nāṭu, ūr, māli, yāṉi, kutiri, koṭi, muracu, āṇi ākiya pattuṟuppu kaḷippaṟṟip pattu nērici veṇpāp pāṭuvatu tacāṅkappattu,
(16) avaṟṟi āciriya viruttattāṟ pāṭuvatu tacāṅkat tayal.
(17) ciṉṉappū: mēṟkūṟiya pattaraca vuṟuppukaḷippaṟṟi, nūṟēṉum toṇṇūṟēṉum eḻupatēṉum aimpatēṉum muppa tēṉum nērici veṇpāp pāṭuvatu ciṉṉappū.
(18) eṇceyyuḷ : aracaṉiṉ peyar talinakar mutaliyavaṟṟip paṟṟi, pattu mutal āyirañ ceyyuḷvari, avvat tokiyāṟ peyarpeṟap pāṭuvatu eṇ ceyyuḷ.
(19) kuṭimaṅkalam: aracaṉatu kuṭiyic ciṟappittu veṇpāvāṟ pāṭuvatu kuṭimaṅkalam.
(20) vāṇmaṅkalam: pakiyaracaṉi veṉṟa vēntaṉ taṉvāḷik koṟṟavimēl niṟutti nīrāṭṭutalik kūṟum ceyyuḷ vāṇmaṅkalam eṉappaṭum.
(21) viḷakku nili: aracaṉatu viḷakku avaṉ ceṅkōlō ṭōṅkivaruvatik kūṟuñ ceyyuḷ viḷakku niliyām.
(22) āṟṟuppaṭi : koṭiyāḷiyāṉa ōr aracaṉiṭattil paricupeṟṟa oru paricilaṉ, taṉ pōlum iṉṉoru paricilaṉi avaṉiṭam āṟṟuppaṭuttuvatāka, akavalāṟ pāṭuvatu āṟṟup paṭi.
iṉi,iravil neṭunēram virittirukkum aracaṉit tuyil koḷḷac collum kaṇpaṭiniliyum, vikaṟiyil avaṉit tuyileḻuppum tuyileṭi niliyum, aṟiñar araṇmaṉi vāyilil niṉṟu tam varavi aracaṟ kaṟivikkumāṟu kaṭikāppaḷa riṭaṅ kūṟum kaṭiniliyum paricilaṉ aracaṉmuṉ niṉṟu, tāṉ karutiya paricil ituveṉak kūṟum pariciṟṟuṟiyum, aracaṉ paricil nīṭṭittavaḻip paricilaṉ taṉ iṭumpikūṟi vēṇṭum paricil kaṭāniliyum, aracaṉ paricilaṉ makiḻap paricaḷittu viṭikoṭut talik kūṟum paricil viṭiyum, paricil koṭutta piṉṉum viṭi koṭukkat tāḻkkum aracaṉiṭattiṉiṉṟu paricilaṉ tāṉē cella oruppaṭutalik kūṟum pariciṉiliyum, aracaṉ iṟanta piṉ paricilar kiyaṟṟup pāṭuṅ kiyaṟuniliyum mutaliya pala tuṟikaḷip paṟṟiya ceyyuḷkaḷum: iyaṉmoḻi puṟanili ceviyaṟivuṟūu vāyuṟi mutaliya palavaki araca vāḻttuc ceyyuḷkaḷum uḷaveṉa aṟika.
- muṟṟiṟṟu. -
